| 1144 | 2188 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 规则 | 学习与可塑性 | Taken together, the measurements support a specific and unglamorous claim: **a recurrent cortical population with a fixed, pre-specified wiring diagram and a purely local correlation rule is already enough to close a sensorimotor loop and to produce behaviour that can be attributed cell population by cell population.** Nothing in the results requires a global objective, a critic, a reward, or a backward pass. |
| 1145 | 2194 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 陈述 | 学习与可塑性? | The ten claims of Section 1.1 are not equally supported, and the difference is the useful part. |
| 1146 | 2195 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 规则 | 学习与可塑性? | Table 1 states, for each of them, the experiment that bears on it and what came out of that experiment; |
| 1147 | 2195 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 证据 | 学习与可塑性? | the last row is not a claim but the question that R4 was built to answer. |
| 1148 | 2198 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 陈述 | 学习与可塑性? | **Table 1. |
| 1149 | 2198 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 规则 | 学习与可塑性? | What each claim rests on.** |
| 1150 | 2214 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 陈述 | 学习与可塑性? | Four sub-claims are worth separating, because they have different evidential status -- one of them, the last, we now report as a negative result. |
| 1151 | 2217 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 陈述 | 学习与可塑性? | *Ordered behaviour without an error signal.* This is the best-supported claim. |
| 1152 | 2217 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 证据 | 学习与可塑性? | It rests on R1 |
| 1153 | 2218 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 规则 | 学习与可塑性? | (five seeds, plus a no-sensation control that collapses), R2 (a population-specific ablation that removes exactly one behaviour), and R3 (a nested sequence of stages in which each addition buys a capability). |
| 1154 | 2222 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 证据 | 本能与标定 | *A repertoire that is assembled rather than trained.* R3 supports the weaker version: adding |
| 1155 | 2223 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 陈述 | 学习与可塑性? | innate structure adds capability without destroying earlier capability, and without any retraining. |
| 1156 | 2223 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 边界与不声明 | 本能与标定 | It does not show a repertoire that *develops*. |
| 1157 | 2223 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 陈述 | 学习与可塑性? | We return to this in 4.3. |
| 1158 | 2226 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 证据 | 学习与可塑性? | *Where a behaviour lives.* R8 is the result that most directly tests the framing the paper starts |
| 1159 | 2227 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 规则 | 学习与可塑性? | It shows both directions: after the stimulus is removed the two codes that carry it read zero while the action is still lit on every tick, and after the cortical state is removed the same wiring still supports the one reflex but no action at all. |
| 1160 | 2227 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 结论 | 学习与可塑性 | The claim that thought is co-activation of a cortical population is therefore not just a description of the diagram; |
| 1161 | 2227 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 陈述 | 学习与可塑性 | removing the co-activation removes exactly the behaviours that the diagram predicts it should, and leaves the rest. |
| 1162 | 2234 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 证据 | 学习与可塑性 | *Local plasticity as the reason that recognition can drift.* R4 supports a bounded version of this, |
| 1163 | 2235 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 陈述 | 学习与可塑性? | and the boundary on the claim is as informative as the claim. |
| 1164 | 2235 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 规则 | 视觉 | The drift moves the recognition of one brain of five on the build of the main table, and all five on the earlier build, and the difference between the two builds is entirely in the draw of random background connectivity -- so the part that is reproducible is not how many brains move but how far: the rule carries a marginal hue across the line and out to the edge of the visual island it belongs to, and never across the gap at 120-144 degrees. |
| 1165 | 2235 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 边界与不声明 | 学习与可塑性? | What it cannot do is create a boundary: with the correlation rule switched off entirely, in a brain that has never drifted, the same five brains accept and refuse exactly the same hues, because the boundary is the value at which two populations sum past threshold, and that value does not depend on the rule. |
| 1166 | 2235 | 4. Discussion | 4.1 What the results support | 规则 | 规模与性能 | This is the result we would most like to see replicated, because it is the one that speaks against reading a fixed recogniser and a correlation rule as interchangeable designs: here they are distinguishable, and what the rule buys is the toleration of drift that is already under way rather than a wider category to begin with. |
| 1167 | 2250 | 4. Discussion | 4.2 What "the last layer" is for | 陈述 | 本能与标定? | The framing we started from says that a layered sensory network is a compressor: many peripheral channels in, few cortical channels out, and the output is a bus rather than an answer. |
| 1168 | 2250 | 4. Discussion | 4.2 What "the last layer" is for | 证据 | 本能与标定? | R5 gives that framing a number. |

| 1170 | 2250 | 4. Discussion | 4.2 What "the last layer" is for | 参数 | 本能与标定 | The compressed layer's direction names are 14% shared at the median and up to 75% shared in the worst columns, where the first two layers are perfectly exclusive. |

| 1172 | 2257 | 4. Discussion | 4.2 What "the last layer" is for | 规则 | 本能与标定? | If one attaches a read-out to the last layer of such a hierarchy and trains it, one is training a classifier on a *deliberately impoverished* signal. |
| 1173 | 2257 | 4. Discussion | 4.2 What "the last layer" is for | 陈述 | 本能与标定? | That is a reasonable engineering choice when the read-out is the point. |

| 1180 | 2267 | 4.
